# Per-infotype XML→predicate translation — the four-layer Rosetta (#428/#430)

> **Status:** working doc (branch `json-data-migration`) · one-time migration artifact (this folder is archived
> out when the migration completes; durable residue lifted via [`TO-BE-MADE-DURABLE.md`](TO-BE-MADE-DURABLE.md)).
> **Date seeded:** 2026-06-22.
> **Grounding:** column 1/3 from [`migration-renames.md`](migration-renames.md) (the rename registry); column 2
> from [`../reference/cascade/legacy-value-calc-map.md`](../reference/cascade/legacy-value-calc-map.md) +
> [`../reference/cascade/glossary.md`](../reference/cascade/glossary.md) (the legacy getters); column 4 from the
> cascade machine refs ([`modifier.md`](../reference/cascade/modifier.md) / [`enabler.md`](../reference/cascade/enabler.md)
> / [`tally.md`](../reference/cascade/tally.md)) + [`data-model.md`](../reference/cascade/data-model.md). Every
> C++ cite is by **function name** — line numbers DRIFT, confirm the function, not the integer.

## Why this exists — "the real takeback" (owner)

The migration touches **four layers at once**, and a curator/`readJson`/cascade change is only safe when you can
trace a single datum across all four. This doc is the **Rosetta Stone**: one row per legacy XML field, mapping it
across

1. **OLD XML tag** — the legacy `Assets/XML` field (what the modder wrote, what `store.py` reads).
2. **MEANS (legacy C++)** — the legacy getter / accumulation site / semantic (what the engine actually DID with it).
3. **NEW JSON key/predicate/target** — the curated `Assets/Data` shape (the rename-registry mapping).
4. **CONSUMING cascade machine** — which of the three machines (enabler / modifier / tally) consumes it, in what
   role (a `requires` gate, a scope-wide deposit, a `plots`-target deposit, a count atom, …).

It is the **durable scope-limiter** for all future curator / `readJson` / cascade work: before touching a field on
any layer, look it up here and confirm the other three. Columns 1+2 are migration-transient (they evaporate at the
atomic cutover when the legacy XML reads are deleted); columns 3+4 are the durable JSON↔cascade map that gets lifted
to the durable set. The [`migration-renames.md`](migration-renames.md) "Predicate vocabulary rewiring" section is
this doc's first content (cols 1+3); this doc adds cols 2+4 and grows to the full per-infotype map.

## How to read a row

- **own-output `plot.flat`** vs **`plots`-target deposit** is THE axis to get right (the plot-substrate slice below):
  - **own-output** (`<yield>.plot.flat`) = the leaf entity's OWN intrinsic yield on the ONE plot it forms/occupies
    (a terrain's grassland food, an improvement's farm food). Deliveryguy = that leaf entity; scope = `plot`.
  - **`plots`-target** (`<yield>.<scope>.plots.flat {predicate}`) = a SCOPE-WIDE source (a building/trait) depositing
    onto **every plot in scope that matches the predicate** (an empire-wide sea buff, a city building's +1 to all its
    worked hills). Realized by evaluating the predicate against every plot in scope and depositing onto each match —
    ONE uniform mechanism, retiring `getSeaPlotYield`/`getYieldChangeAt`/the per-plot-type accumulators
    ([`data-model.md` §4.1](../reference/cascade/data-model.md)).
- **Predicates are target-relative, no `_PLOT`/`_UNIT` suffix.** The vocabulary itself is NOT re-enumerated here —
  it is the authoring authority's ([modder `datastructure/README.md` §3.4/§6](../../modders/datastructure/README.md) +
  [`data-model.md` §2.5](../reference/cascade/data-model.md)); this doc only **applies** it per field (lockstep —
  never duplicate the vocabulary list).

---

## Section 1 — PLOT SUBSTRATE & the `plots` target (the predicate-rewiring locus)

The fields that map to a plot **predicate** or the **`plots` target**. This is the active rewiring (owner 2026-06-22:
the `IS_*`/`HAS_*` split + the `plots` target; `plotTypes`/`seaPlot` dissolved).

### 1a. Own-output plot yields (the leaf carries its own `plot.flat`)

| OLD XML (entity) | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Yields` (Terrain) | terrain base tile yield → `CvPlot::calculateYield`/`calculateNatureYield` (terrain part), summed into `m_aiBaseYieldRate` | `<yield>.plot.flat` (food/production/commerce) | modifier — `plot`-scope own-output (deliveryguy = terrain) |
| `YieldChanges` (Feature) | feature per-plot DELTA, `CvPlot::calculateYield` (`+=` feature) | `<yield>.plot.flat` | modifier — `plot` own-output |
| `YieldChanges` (Improvement) | `calculateImprovementYieldChange` (the `impX` component: base/river/route/tech/civic upgrades) | `<yield>.plot.flat` | modifier — `plot` own-output |
| `Yields` (Route) | route tile yield in `CvPlot::calculateYield` (route part) | `<yield>.plot.flat` | modifier — `plot` own-output |
| `YieldChanges` (Bonus) | `calculateNatureYield` bonus part (`bonus.getYieldChange`) — the on-map resource buffs its tile downward | `<yield>.plot.flat` | modifier — `plot` own-output (bonus sits above the plot in the spine, deposits down) |

### 1b. Conditional own-output plot yields (own-output, predicate-gated)

| OLD XML (entity) | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| `YieldInfo.iRiverChange` synthetic (Terrain) | the live `CvPlot` river-boolean adds a commerce on river-capable land terrains (Info data is the conditioner, the plot's live river flag is the fact) | `<commerce>.plot.flat[].{value, enabled:"HAS_RIVER"}` (the 19 river-capable land terrains) | modifier — `plot` own-output, `enabled: HAS_RIVER` predicate (1st real `HAS_RIVER` use) |
| `YieldInfo.iHillsChange`/`iPeakChange` synthetic (Terrain) | `YieldInfo.getHillsChange`/`getPeakChange` plot-type deltas, re-homed onto `TERRAIN_HILL`/`TERRAIN_PEAK` (dual plot-type+terrain entities) | `<yield>.plot.flat` on `TERRAIN_HILL`/`PEAK` (PEAK commerce is a mixed list `[-1, {value:1, enabled:"HAS_RIVER"}]`) | modifier — `plot` own-output (NOT a predicate: they ARE those terrains) |
| `RiverYieldChange` (Feature) | `getRiverYieldChange` — feature's extra river-side yield (forest-on-river) | `<yield>.plot.flat[].{value, enabled:"HAS_RIVER"}` | modifier — `plot` own-output, `enabled: HAS_RIVER` |
| `RiverSideYieldChange` (Improvement) | extra yield on a river plot | `<yield>.plot.flat[].{value, enabled:"HAS_RIVER"}` | modifier — `plot` own-output, `enabled: HAS_RIVER` |
| `IrrigatedYieldChange` (Improvement) | extra yield when the plot is irrigated | `<yield>.plot.flat[].{value, enabled:"HAS_IRRIGATION"}` | modifier — `plot` own-output, `enabled: HAS_IRRIGATION` |
| `BonusTypeStructs.YieldChanges` (Improvement) | per-bonus extra yield (mine-on-coal) | `<yield>.plot.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_BONUS: BONUS_X}}` | modifier — `plot` own-output, parameterized `HAS_BONUS` |

### 1c. THE FOLDS — scope-wide `plots`-target deposits (`plotTypes`/`seaPlot` → `plots {predicate}`)

✅ **LANDED + REGENERATED 2026-06-22** — the curators emit the `plots` target via `_inject_plots` (building) / the trait handler; `plotTypes`/`seaPlot`/`.river` = **0** in `Assets/Data`, `plots` present (38 buildings + 4 traits). The per-row "◐ PENDING" notes below are the *pre-landing* curator-site reference, kept for traceability. Each is a scope-wide source depositing onto **every matching plot in scope**.

| OLD XML (entity) | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PlotYieldChanges` (Building) | per-plot-TYPE map (OCEAN/LAND/HILLS/PEAK) → `getYieldChangeAt` (the `wcX` working-city component), applied per worked tile of that type via `CvPlot::calculateYield` workingCityYieldChange | `<yield>.<scope>.plots.flat {IS_WATER\|IS_LAND\|HAS_HILLS\|HAS_PEAK}` | modifier — `plots`-target (retires `getYieldChangeAt` + the per-plot-type accumulators) | ◐ PENDING (curator `TARGET_KEYED` still `plotTypes`, `curate_building.py:275`) |
| `GlobalSeaPlotYieldChanges` (Building) | `getSeaPlotYield` (the `seaX` component — player flat bonus to water tiles, Lighthouse/Harbor) | `<yield>.empire.plots.flat {IS_WATER}` | modifier — `plots`-target (retires `getSeaPlotYield`) | ◐ PENDING (`YIELD_FAMILIES` still `empire.flat`+post_process, `curate_building.py:141`) |
| `RiverPlotYieldChanges` (Building) | `getRiverPlotYield` — building yield to river plots | `<yield>.<scope>.plots.flat {HAS_RIVER}` | modifier — `plots`-target | ◐ PENDING; ✅ **owner-confirmed 2026-06-22: FOLD** (`.river` does NOT stay), `curate_building.py:142` |
| `SeaPlotYieldChanges` (Trait) | player sea-plot yield, empire-wide | `<yield>.empire.plots.flat {IS_WATER}` | modifier — `plots`-target | ◐ PENDING (`KEYED` still `empire.seaPlot.flat`, `curate_trait.py:170`) |

`plotTypes` and the bespoke `seaPlot` key are **RETIRED**. Named-entity keys (`terrains`/`improvements`/`features`)
STAY (deliveryguy, [`modifier.md` §6](../reference/cascade/modifier.md)) — a plot **fact** → a `plots`-target
predicate; a **named entity** → its entity-key.

### 1d. Placement / build-gate plot predicates (`requires.build`, the enabler side)

| OLD XML (entity) | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bWaterImprovement` (Improvement) | domain gate, `CvPlot::canHaveImprovement` | `requires.build.all: ["IS_WATER"]` | enabler — `requires.build` (greying) |
| `bPeakImprovement` / `bPeakMakesValid` (Improvement) | peak domain / make-valid | `IS_WATER`-peer `"HAS_PEAK"` in `build.all` / `build.any` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bRequiresIrrigation` (Improvement) | `isIrrigationAvailable` check | `"HAS_IRRIGATION"` in `build.all` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bRequiresFlatlands` (Improvement) | flat-plot gate | `"IS_FLATLANDS"` in `build.all` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bRequiresFeature` (Improvement) | has-any-feature gate | `"HAS_FEATURE"` in `build.all` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bCanMoveSeaUnits` (Improvement) | coastal-land gate (legacy `COASTAL_LAND`) | `["IS_LAND","HAS_COAST"]` in `build.all` (composite, no bespoke token) | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bRequiresRiverSide` / `bRiverSideMakesValid` (Improvement) | river-plot gate (`isRequiresRiverSide`, DllExport) | `"HAS_RIVER"` in `build.all`/`build.any` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bHillsMakesValid` (Improvement) | hills make-valid | `"HAS_HILLS"` in `build.any` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bFreshWaterMakesValid` / `bNoFreshWater` (Improvement) | freshwater make-valid / forbid | `"HAS_FRESHWATER"` in `build.any` / `build.noneOf` | enabler — `requires.build` (positive / dormancy-negative) |
| `bWater` (Building) | `isValidBuildingLocation` gates on `isCoastal()` (NOT plot `isWater` — a city sits on land) | `"HAS_COAST"` in `build.all` (city-context `HAS_COAST` = `isCoastal`) | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bWater ∧ bRiver` (Building) | coastal OR river (`isValidBuildingLocation`) | `build.any: [["HAS_COAST","HAS_RIVER"]]` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bRiver` (Building) | river-plot gate | `"HAS_RIVER"` in `build.all` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `bFreshWater` (Building) | freshwater gate | `"HAS_FRESHWATER"` in `build.all` | enabler — `requires.build` |
| `Is TAG_COASTAL` (BoolExpr: `NewCityFree`) | `CvCity::isCoastal` | bare `"HAS_COAST"` (target-relative: city-context = `isCoastal`) | enabler/modifier — `enabled`/`requires` clause |

**Target-relativity (owner-confirmed 2026-06-22):** `HAS_COAST` on a `plots` deposit = a plot adjacent to water; in
a building/city context (`requires.build`, `NewCityFree`) = the city's own `isCoastal` fact — **NOT** a center-plot
check ("we should not need a building to know if there's coast on the city plot"). The engine/`readJson` resolves the
predicate per the deposit's target.

## Section 2 — Tier A: config / global axes

### BonusClass

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| iUniqueRange | CvBonusClassInfo::getUniqueRange() — read at world generation by CvMapGenerator (the addBonusType placement loop, getUniqueRange() call sites ~CvMapGenerator:69/101): the class-level min-spacing that, combined with the bonus's own range, prevents same-class bonuses stacking in close proximity. A C2C_World mapscript placement constraint; NOT read by any per-turn engine value getter. | mapGeneration.uniqueRange (int; 0 = no constraint, dropped — bare {type} emitted) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — map-generation placement spacing; consumed by CvMapGenerator at world-gen, not by any of the three cascade machines (modifier/enabler/tally read only the cascade sections per data-model.md). Parallels the BONUS's own mapGeneration.uniqueRange. |

### CivicOption (curate_civicoption.py — EntityConfig("CivicOptionInfo"), thin config over curate_common, empty boosts)

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| <Type> | CvInfoBase::getType() — the type string; consumed engine-side as the CivicOptionTypes enum index that keys the per-slot active civic (CvPlayer::getCivics(CivicOptionTypes)/setCivics(CivicOptionTypes,CivicTypes), e.g. CvPlayer.cpp init at line 480/1535 and the canDoCivics enforce loop at 4089/4097). Civics page grouped by it Python-side (getCivicOptionType, CivicData.py). | type (identity-by-filename: dropped from the authored body per cfg.drop = {"Type"}; survives as the JSON "type" key + the file name civicoption_<short>.json) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — pure structural civic-SLOT axis; the cascade consumption is CIVIC-side (each Civic carries its CivicOptionType), not on this entity |
| <Description> | CvInfoBase::getDescription() — the TXT_KEY display string; HOIST maps Description -> text_fields, emitted as out["description"]. Display only (civic-screen slot heading); not read by any game-logic accumulator. | description | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — display text |

### GameSpeed

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| <Type> | the GameSpeedInfo type key — the entity identity discriminator (engine resolves via GC.getInfoTypeForString / getGameSpeedInfo(eGameSpeed)); not a value the modifier/enabler/tally machines consume | type | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| <Description> | display text — CvGameSpeedInfo::getDescription / getText; empire-agnostic self-description, read by UI/pedia only | description | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| <Help> | civilopedia/help text — CvGameSpeedInfo getText('Help'); UI/pedia only | help | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| <iSpeedPercent> | the master game-pace percentage. Engine reads it via CvGameSpeedInfo::getSpeedPercent and getHammerCostPercent (= speedPercent x UPSCALED-if-option); the SAME value scales research/anarchy (getResearchCost, getCivicAnarchyLength) and is applied across costs/durations/growth/cultureThreshold (the §7 combine-mode multiplier; unit=percent, e.g. Normal=100, Eternity=1000). Per legacy-value-calc-map: 'hammerCostPercent = gamespeed.speedPercent x(UPSCALED if option)'; 'speedPercent (research/anarchy)' | speed.world.percent | modifier — world-scope deposit (the master pacing multiplier sitting at the TOP of the cascade, scaling everything beneath; combine-mode carries the multiplicative math, value unit=percent) |
| <iUnitYieldScalePercent> | the percentage multiplier on yields a unit MISSION produces (merchant trade-mission, subdued-animal slaughter). Engine reads it via CvGameSpeedInfo::getUnitYieldScalePercent, applied by adaptValueToGame(ADAPT_UNIT_YIELD) = value x gamespeed.getUnitYieldScalePercent / 100 (the <AdaptUnitYield> channel, ~sqrt of speed; Normal=500, Eternity=1575) | missionYieldMultiplier.world.percent | modifier — world-scope deposit (independently-tunable mission-yield multiplier; unit=percent) |

### Hurry

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| iGoldPerProduction | GC.getHurryInfo(eHurry).getGoldPerProduction() — gold-rush BASE rate; read in CvCity::getHurryGold (iGold = iHurryCost * getGoldPerProduction()) and CvCity::hurry/canHurry to detect a gold-rush (getGoldPerProduction()>0 => bBuy). The cascade modifier on hurry COST is getHurryCostModifier (BuildingInfo.iHurryCostModifier via getModifiedIntValue), which acts ON this base rate — this tag is the intrinsic base, not a modifier. | conversion.goldPerProduction | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — intrinsic rush BASE rate; the cascade hurry-cost modifier (BuildingInfo.hurryCostModifier, city-scope percent) deposits/acts elsewhere, not here. 0 (not-applicable) is dropped — its absence reconstructs as 0, which is how the engine distinguishes gold-rush from pop-rush (getX()>0). |
| iProductionPerPopulation | GC.getHurryInfo(eHurry).getProductionPerPopulation() (via GC.getGame().getProductionPerPopulation(eHurry)) — pop/slavery-rush BASE rate; read in CvCity::getHurryPopulation (hammers->population: 1 + (iHurryCost-1)/prodPerPop), CvCity::hurryProduction (population->hammers), and CvCity::hurry/canHurry/hurryOverflow to detect a pop-rush (getProductionPerPopulation()>0 => bWhip). | conversion.productionPerPopulation | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — intrinsic rush BASE rate; mutually exclusive with goldPerProduction per hurry. 0 dropped (reconstructs as 0). Not a deposited modifier (curator docstring lines 11-12). |
| bAnger | GC.getHurryInfo(eHurry).isAnger() — read in CvCity::hurryAngerLength (isAnger() ? flatHurryAngerLength() : 0); when set, using the hurry starts changeHurryAngerTimer in CvCity::hurry, feeding getHurryPercentAnger -> getAngerPercent (temporary hurry-anger unhappiness). | causesAnger | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — intrinsic mechanic flag; gates whether using this hurry inflicts the temporary hurry-anger duration. The anger MAGNITUDE/duration math (flatHurryAngerLength, getHurryAngerModifier) lives on CvCity, not on this entity; this is just the on/off flag. Emitted only when true (false dropped). |

| bDiploVote | isDiploVote() — read by CvGame::testVictory (UN/diplomatic-vote win) | condition.diploVote (true only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| bTotalVictory | isTotalVictory() — read by CvGame::testVictory (mastery/total win) | condition.totalVictory (true only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| bPermanent | isPermanent() — DllExport (EXE-bound) + Python-consumed (PbWizard.py, CvWBDesc.py); the always-available score victory | condition.permanent (true only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`; DllExport HARD EXE LINK, also Python; no cascade machine |
| CityCulture | getCityCulture() — a CultureLevel ref (enum-as-int); the culture level each of numCultureCities must reach (cultural win) | condition.cityCulture (CULTURELEVEL_* ref; emitted unless NONE) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory; forward ref to CultureLevel |
| iLandPercent | getLandPercent() — read by CvGame::testVictory (domination land-area threshold) | condition.landPercent (non-zero only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| iMinLandPercent | getMinLandPercent() — read by CvGame::testVictory (domination minimum land floor) | condition.minLandPercent (non-zero only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| iPopulationPercentLead | getPopulationPercentLead() — read by CvGame::testVictory (domination population-lead threshold) | condition.populationPercentLead (non-zero only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| iReligionPercent | getReligionPercent() — read by CvGame::testVictory (religious-win majority threshold) | condition.religionPercent (non-zero only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| iNumCultureCities | getNumCultureCities() — read by CvGame::testVictory (cultural-win count of qualifying cities) | condition.numCultureCities (non-zero only) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| iVictoryDelayTurns | getVictoryDelayTurns() — read by CvGame::testVictory (space-race travel delay before the win triggers) | condition.delayTurns (non-zero only; dropped redundant Victory prefix) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — bespoke `condition`, read by testVictory |
| VictoryMovie | getMovie() — victory cinematic art path | ui.art.movie.file (via put_art/emit_art ART_BLOCK) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — art/UI |

### Vote

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| Description | getDescription / getText (display name of the resolution; no engine logic) | description | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| DiploVotes\DiploVote (DIPLOVOTE_UN / DIPLOVOTE_POPE / DIPLOVOTE_CVIENNA) | isVoteSourceType(i) — which VoteSource/council may RAISE this proposal; read at doDiploVote / countSecretaryGeneral loops (CvGame.cpp:2995,3011,3026,9338). Intrinsic eligibility, NOT a cascade enabler gate. | voteSource | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iPopulationThreshold | getPopulationThreshold — % of eligible votes needed to pass; countVote: countPossibleVote()*getPopulationThreshold()/100 (CvGame.cpp:2963) | threshold.population | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iMinVoters | getMinVoters — minimum eligible voters for the resolution to be valid (CvGame.cpp:4247) | threshold.minVoters | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iStateReligionVotePercent | getStateReligionVotePercent — per-player vote-weight bonus % when state religion matches the vote source (getter confirmed CvVoteInfo.h:24); per curator docstring | threshold.stateReligionPercent | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bSecretaryGeneral | isSecretaryGeneral — marks this as the SG/Pope/Chair ELECTION resolution class (CvGame.cpp:2843,2996,9729). Resolution CLASS, not an on-pass effect. | role = secretaryGeneral | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bVictory | isVictory — marks this as the diplo-VICTORY resolution class; checked at vote-result/victory eval (CvGame.cpp:2843,4178,7603) | role = victory | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bFreeTrade | isFreeTrade — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote: changeFreeTradeCount(isFreeTrade()?iChange:0) (CvGame.cpp:7956). Not a modifier/enabler. | effect.freeTrade | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bNoNukes | isNoNukes — on-pass OUTCOME: processVote changeNoNukesCount(isNoNukes()?iChange:0) (CvGame.cpp:7957) | effect.noNukes | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bDefensivePact | isDefensivePact — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (forces team defensive pacts) (CvGame.cpp:7986; eval 4275) | effect.defensivePact | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bOpenBorders | isOpenBorders — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (forces team open borders) (CvGame.cpp:7968; eval 4252) | effect.openBorders | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bForcePeace | isForcePeace — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (forces peace) (CvGame.cpp:8004; eval 4301) | effect.forcePeace | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bForceNoTrade | isForceNoTrade — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (forces trade embargo) (CvGame.cpp:8020; eval 4332) | effect.forceNoTrade | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bForceWar | isForceWar — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (forces war) (CvGame.cpp:8038; eval 4356) | effect.forceWar | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bAssignCity | isAssignCity — on-pass OUTCOME applied by processVote (assigns a city) (CvGame.cpp:8055; eval 4412; triggered-data 5736) | effect.assignCity | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iTradeRoutes | getTradeRoutes — on-pass OUTCOME: processVote changeTradeRoutes(getTradeRoutes()*iChange) adds to the game trade-route pool (CvGame.cpp:7955). An outcome, NOT a cascade modifier deposit. | effect.tradeRoutes | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| ForceCivics\ForceCivic | isForceCivic(i) — on-pass OUTCOME: processVote changeForceCivicCount((CivicTypes)iI, isForceCivic(iI)?iChange:0) forces the listed civic(s) (CvGame.cpp:7961,5192) | effect.forceCivics | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bCityVoting | isCityVoting — tally MODE flag read by CvPlayer::getVotes (per curator docstring; getter confirmed CvVoteInfo.h:32). All-zero in data; emitted only if true. | mode.cityVoting | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bCivVoting | isCivVoting — tally MODE flag read by CvPlayer::getVotes (per curator docstring; getter confirmed CvVoteInfo.h:33). All-zero in data; emitted only if true. | mode.civVoting | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |

| iHappiness | getHappiness — per-city corp happiness, summed into happyLevel/unhappyLevel signed-split while corp active (applyCorporationModifiers) | happiness.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}} | modifier — city deposit (gated by HAS_CORPORATION) |
| iFreeXP | getFreeXP — per-city free experience granted to units built while corp active (applyCorporationModifiers) | experience.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}} | modifier — city deposit (gated by HAS_CORPORATION) |
| iMilitaryProductionModifier | getMilitaryProductionModifier — percent production mod on military units in a corp-active city (cost-style via getModifiedIntValue, applyCorporationModifiers) | production.city.military.percent[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}} | modifier — city deposit (military member, percent; gated by HAS_CORPORATION) |
| iMaintenance | getMaintenance — per-owned-prereq-bonus maintenance rate, accumulated in calculateCorporationMaintenanceTimes100 (cost-style getModifiedIntValue) | maintenance.city.corporation.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}, per:{anyOf:[prereqBonuses], scope:city}} | modifier — city deposit (corporation member, per-bonus scaled; gated by HAS_CORPORATION). TODO: value likely x100 (calculateCorporationMaintenanceTimes100) — not yet de-scaled. |
| CommerceChanges (split per-commerce: COMMERCE_GOLD/RESEARCH/CULTURE/ESPIONAGE) | getCommerceChange / getCorporationCommerce — genuinely-flat per-city commerce add while corp active; x1 (getCommerceChange is x100'd in-formula). Family identifier IS the commerce name | <commerce-family>.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}} | modifier — city deposit, SPLIT base commerce families (gated by HAS_CORPORATION) |
| YieldChanges (split per-yield: YIELD_FOOD/PRODUCTION/COMMERCE) | getYieldChange / getCorporationYield — genuinely-flat per-city yield add while corp active; x1 (getYieldChange is x100'd in-formula). Family identifier IS the yield name | <yield-family>.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}} | modifier — city deposit, SPLIT base yield families (gated by HAS_CORPORATION) |
| CommercesProduced (split per-commerce) | getCommerceProduced — per-owned-resource commerce output: CommerceProduced x SUM getNumBonuses(prereqBonus); getCorporationCommerceByCorporation makes produced x100 (75 -> 0.75/bonus) | <commerce-family>.city.flat[].{value(de-scaled /100), enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}, per:{anyOf:[prereqBonuses], scope:city}} | modifier — city deposit, SPLIT commerce families, per-bonus scaled (gated by HAS_CORPORATION). value x100 in XML -> de-scaled to human |
| YieldsProduced (split per-yield) | getYieldProduced — per-owned-resource yield output: YieldProduced x SUM getNumBonuses(prereqBonus); getCorporationYieldByCorporation (CvCity.cpp:12594-12602) makes produced x100 | <yield-family>.city.flat[].{value(de-scaled /100), enabled:{HAS_CORPORATION:SELF}, per:{anyOf:[prereqBonuses], scope:city}} | modifier — city deposit, SPLIT yield families, per-bonus scaled (gated by HAS_CORPORATION). value x100 in XML -> de-scaled to human |
| HeadquarterCommerces (split per-commerce) | getHeadquarterCommerce — HQ-revenue lever; empire-wide, feeds corp maintenance/tax + a HQ building's globalCorporationCommerce x countCorporationLevels | <commerce-family>.empire.headquarters.perCorporationLevel (UNCHANGED, ungated) | modifier — empire deposit (headquarters member, perCorporationLevel unit). DEFERRED to corp rework pass: HQ-city modeling + per-corp-level token pending; kept in current form, not yet gated |
| iSpreadCost | getSpreadCost — intrinsic base GOLD cost to spread the corp (distinct from GameSpeed/Era costs-multiplier family) | cost.spread | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — a cost section (intrinsic spread gold cost) |
| BonusProduced | getBonusProduced — free resource provided in cities where the corp is active | grants.bonusProduced | grants |
| FreeUnit | getFreeUnit — a free unit handed out (provision); curator GRANTS map | grants.freeUnit | grants |
| iSpread | getSpread — influence->spread % (spread-probability config), matched/read, not summed | identity.spread | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — spread config |
| iSpreadFactor | getSpreadFactor — spread-unit cost scaling config | identity.spreadFactor | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — spread config |
| Categories | getCategories — corp category membership array (matched by attribute, not summed); none in base XML | identity.categories | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| CompetingCorporations | isCompetingCorporation — corp<->corp mutual-exclusion set (matched, not summed); none in base XML | identity.competingCorporations | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| TechPrereq | getTechPrereq — the generating tech; the corp is generated by its tech (store inverts the edge onto the tech, not authored on the corp) | (store) tech.enables.corporations — DROPPED from the corp record | enabler — enables (source-side edge authored on the TECH, not the corp) |
| PrereqBonuses (BonusType list) | getPrereqBonus — the resource set scaling *Produced/iMaintenance output (SUM getNumBonuses) + the FOUND requirement (authored on the HQ FoundsCorporation building) | (read into per:{anyOf:[prereqBonuses], scope:city}) + HQ building requires.build (Building pass) — DROPPED as a corp edge | tally — per-scaler basis (per:anyOf over the bonus set) feeding the *Produced/maintenance deposits; the FOUND gate is an enabler requires.build on the HQ building (Building pass), NOT a corp edge |
| PrereqBuildings | getPrereqBuilding — means-not-generator (store row removed; relates to HQ building) | (store row removed; -> Building/HQ) — DROPPED | enabler — NOT a corp edge (deferred to Building/HQ pass) |
| (derived) store.enabled_by(corp) <- BuildingInfo.PrereqCorporation | isPrereqCorporation on the target building — a building gated by the active corp; inverted top-down by the store into the corp's enables edge | enables.buildings | enabler — enables (the corp unlocks buildings that require the active corp) |
| Button / MovieFile / MovieSound / Sound / iTGAIndex | art/sound FK getters (getButton / getMovie / getSound / getTGAIndex); asset references, never engine math | ui.art.* / world.* / sound.* (via curate_common.ART_BLOCK) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — art/sound blocks |

### Religion

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| StateReligionCommerces (per-commerce array: gold/research/culture/espionage) | Each commerce value deposited into a city that HAS this religion, gated by the state-religion compound (present AND (is-state-religion OR no-state-religion OR non-state commerce)); accumulated through the city's commerce path — getCommerceRate100 via isHasReligion / getStateReligion (CvCity getAdditionalCommerceByReligion ~12465, isHasReligion at 14880) | <commerce>.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{STATE_RELIGION: RELIGION_X}} — split per-commerce family (gold/research/culture/espionage); each commerce IS its own family member | modifier — city-scope flat deposit, conditioned by object-evaluated predicate STATE_RELIGION (parameterized predicate keeps object form per modifier.md §BARE-STRING) |
| HolyCityCommerces (per-commerce array) | Each commerce value deposited only in this religion's holy city; gated by isHolyCity(eReligion); accumulated through the city commerce path — getCommerceRate100 via isHolyCity (CvCity isHolyCity ~5297; getAdditionalCommerceByReligion ~12469) | <commerce>.city.flat[].{value, enabled:{HOLY_CITY: RELIGION_X}} — split per-commerce family; multiple conditioned deposits to one commerce/scope/unit form a LIST (modifier-spec §1.3) | modifier — city-scope flat deposit, conditioned by object-evaluated predicate HOLY_CITY (city self-reports isHolyCity) |
| GlobalReligionCommerces (per-commerce array) | The religion's SHRINE contribution: getGlobalReligionCommerce(eIndex) * countReligionLevels(religion), WORLD-scaled, consumed wherever a building's getGlobalReligionCommerce() points at this religion (CvCity getAdditionalCommerce... ~12146/12249, countReligionLevels) | shrine.{commerce} — raw per-commerce ints PARKED to the Building pass (Building wires the world-scaling via countReligionLevels + the religion<->shrine-building routing) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) at the Religion pass — raw values parked; the actual modifier deposit (world-scaled shrine contribution) is authored at the Building pass, not consumed from the religion JSON directly |
| BuildingInfo.ReligionChanges (inbound, per-specific-religion-keyed; NOT a ReligionInfo XML tag) | A building delivers per-religion influence into its city: changeReligionInfluence(eIndex, value*iChange) accumulated into getReligionInfluence (CvCity changeReligionInfluence ~14113, applied ~4705) | NOT inverted onto the religion (owner 2026-06-20, modifier.md §6.5): stays ON THE BUILDING keyed by religion (curate_building TARGET_KEYED 'ReligionChanges'). The curator's religionInfluence family / RELIGION_BOOSTS fold is currently EMPTY (RELIGION_BOOSTS = []) | modifier — deliverer effect lives on the source building (keep-on-source / deliveryguy ownership); the religion is only the key, not the deposit holder |
| PrereqReligion (on BuildingInfo / UnitInfo — store-derived, religion is the conditioner) | A building/unit construct/train gate: kBuilding.getPrereqReligion()/kUnit.getPrereqReligion() != NO_RELIGION && !isHasReligion(eReligion) blocks (CvCity canConstruct ~2618/2620; canTrain ~2182) — the religion is the GOM_RELIGION conditioner, parallel to PrereqCorporation | religion.enables.{buildings,units} — derived from the store (store.enabled_by(typ)); the religion is the source-side conditioner whose presence unlocks those targets | enabler — enables (source-side GENERATOR edge, read forward over HAS; the religion in HAS adds those buildings/units to CAN GET) |
| FreeUnit + iFreeUnits | Religion-founding award: a free unit granted, gated ENTIRELY by the count — `if getNumFreeUnits() > 0` (curator docstring cites CvPlayer.cpp:8838); when iFreeUnits==0 the FreeUnit FK is inert and the game grants nothing | grants.freeUnit (FK) + grants.numFreeUnits (count); freeUnit emitted ONLY when numFreeUnits>0 — the FK with count 0 is dropped (~13 religions have FreeUnit set but iFreeUnits=0) | grants — one-shot provision (top-down, non-cascading; data-model §3.5) |
| iSpreadFactor | Per-religion spread-decay scalar used by the religion-spread probability logic; read directly, never summed into a value channel | identity.spreadFactor | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| TechPrereq | The tech that enables founding this religion | DROPPED on the religion — store inverts it to tech.enables.religions (the tech is the generator, kept there) | enabler — enables edge authored on the TECH (forward over HAS from the tech), not on the religion; dropped from the religion JSON |
| PropertyManipulators / PropertySource | Per-entity property deposits (CONSTANT / relation sources); religions carry NONE in base XML — defensive no-op (engine.property_source_v3) | <PROPERTY>.<scope>.<unit> (v3) — emitted only if present; none in base XML so never appears | modifier — property-family deposit (defensive; not populated for religions) |
| Description / Civilopedia | Display text (TXT_KEY resolved) | description / civilopedia | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — text |
| Adjective | Display adjective text (TXT_KEY) | adjective | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — text |
| Flavors (Flavor/iFlavor list) | AI weighting flavors | ai.flavours | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — AI-only metadata |
| Button / TechButton / GenericTechButton / MovieFile / MovieSound / Sound / iTGAIndex | Art / UI / movie / sound / icon-index asset FKs | routed to ui/world/sound blocks via curate_common.ART_BLOCK (cc.put_art / cc.emit_art); empty/NONE dropped | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — art/audio assets |

## Section 4 — Tier C: resources / map substrate (non-plot fields; plot-substrate is Section 1)

### Bonus

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| iHealth | CvBonusInfo::getHealth() — accumulated in CvCity::processBonus: changeBonusGoodHealth(GC.getBonusInfo(eBonus).getHealth() * iChange) summing into m_iBonusGoodHealth (the connected-resource health contribution to the city health total). Reader CvCity::getBonusHealth(eBonus). | health.empire.flat | modifier — empire-scope deposit (own amplification; the trade-connected resource's empire-wide health benefit, building-cascade-conversion.md:1205-1207) |
| iHappiness | CvBonusInfo::getHappiness() — accumulated in CvCity::processBonus: changeBonusGoodHappiness(GC.getBonusInfo(eBonus).getHappiness() * iChange) summing into m_iBonusGoodHappiness. Reader CvCity::getBonusHappiness(eBonus). | happiness.empire.flat | modifier — empire-scope deposit (own amplification; connected-resource empire-wide happiness benefit) |

## Section 5 — Tier D: producers / unit-plane sources

### Heritage

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| <EraCommerceChanges><EraType> + <CentiCommerce>{Gold/Research/Culture/Espionage} | processHeritage iterates getEraCommerceChanges100(), gates each band with `if (eEra >= pair.first)` against getCurrentEra() (a THRESHOLD, bands accumulate), and deposits via changeExtraCommerce100 (read back through CvPlayer::getCommerceRate100). Cross-check getter: getHeritageCommerceEraChange. | <commerceFamily>.empire.byEra.<C2C_ERA_*>.flat — split into gold/research/culture/espionage families; era key is the era Type verbatim; CentiCommerce x100 de-scaled to human (descale100). | modifier — empire-scope deposit (commerce families, byEra = a threshold condition on currentEra, not a scope) |
| <PropertyManipulators><PropertySource ...> (PROPERTYSOURCE_CONSTANT iAmountPerTurn / PROPERTYSOURCE_DECAY iPercent / ATTRIBUTE-scaled / <Active> BoolExpr e.g. {GOM_TECH:...}) | Authored on CvPropertyInfo::m_PropertyManipulators; the realized per-turn value is the property solver CvPropertySolver::gatherAndSolve (sources phase) read via CvProperties::getValueByProperty (legacy-value-calc-map §9.1) — not a bare engine getter on the heritage. | PROPERTY_<X>.<scope>.<unit> via engine.property_source_v3 — CONSTANT->flat, DECAY->percent, attribute->per:{type,each,scope}, Active BoolExpr->enabled; scope from GameObjectType (default city); multiple sources to the same (prop,scope,unit) leaf accumulate as a LIST. | modifier — property deposit (scope per source, default city; list-of-entries when several gated sources hit one leaf) |
| <PrereqTech> | canAddHeritage gate — `if (!GET_TEAM(getTeam()).isHasTech((TechTypes)heritage.getPrereqTech())) return false`. | DROPPED on the heritage; inverted top-down to the tech's enables.heritages (tech.enables grants the heritage). | enabler — enables (the inverse edge lives on the TECH as tech.enables.heritages; not emitted on the heritage) |
| <PrereqOrHeritage> | canAddHeritage gate — foreach getPrereqOrHeritage(): require hasHeritage(one of them) (folklore->taxon succession). (Same field also gates building/unit construction via canConstruct/canTrain getPrereqOrHeritage / getPrereqAndHeritage.) | DROPPED on the heritage; the predecessor heritage's enables.heritages block is derived from this succession edge (store.enabled_by reverse index) and emitted as the predecessor's `enables`. | enabler — enables (derived heritage->heritage succession edge; emitted on the ENABLING heritage's `enables.heritages`) |
| (derived — no own XML tag) heritage `enables` block | Reverse index of other heritages' PrereqOrHeritage edges (store.enabled_by), mirroring the canAddHeritage getPrereqOrHeritage succession gate. | enables: { heritages: [...] } (sorted) — only present when this heritage is a prereq-OR for another. | enabler — enables (top-down succession edge generated from the inverted prereq) |
| <bNeedLanguage> | canAddHeritage gate — `if (heritage.needLanguage() && !m_bHasLanguage) return false` (a player-state acquisition gate; no source entity to invert the edge onto). | identity.needsLanguage: true (PRESERVED, flagged — kept until the prereq-model reconciliation). | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — a player-state acquisition gate parked on identity; NOT yet a cascade enabler edge (flagged) |
| <Description> | Display text (TXT_KEY_* resolved); not consumed by any value calc. | description (top-level text field) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — text |
| <Civilopedia> | Civilopedia display text; not consumed by any value calc. | civilopedia (top-level text field) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — text |
| <Button> | UI icon path; not consumed by any value calc. | ui.art.icon (via cc.put_art ART_BLOCK / emit_art) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — art |
| <Type> | The entity's own Type key (the HeritageInfo identifier). | type | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — self-identifier |

|---|---|---|---|
| iNukeInterception | CvTeam::processProjectChange -> changeNukeInterception (CvTeam.cpp:4513); team-scope nuke-intercept chance | combat.team.nukeInterception.percent | modifier — team deposit (provisional family/member name per curator docstring) |

| iDistanceMaintenanceModifier | processProjectChange empire branch; distance-maintenance component of getEffectiveMaintenanceModifier (getMaintenanceTimes100, CvCity.cpp:7579) | maintenance.empire.distance.percent | modifier — empire deposit (cost-style combine) |

| iInflationModifier | processProjectChange empire branch; m_iInflationModifier source-component summed in getInflationMod10000 (CvPlayer.cpp:7965/8008) -> getProjectInflation | upkeep.empire.inflation.percent | modifier — empire deposit (cost-style combine) |
| iGlobalHappiness | processProjectChange empire branch (per-player on team); empire-scope flat happiness delta | happiness.empire.flat | modifier — empire deposit |
| iGlobalHealth | processProjectChange empire branch (per-player on team); empire-scope flat health delta | health.empire.flat | modifier — empire deposit |
| iWorldHappiness | processProjectChange WORLD branch (CvTeam.cpp:4572-4574, OUTSIDE the team check, EVERY alive player); world-scope flat happiness | happiness.world.flat | modifier — world deposit |
| iWorldHealth | processProjectChange WORLD branch (CvTeam.cpp:4572-4574, every alive player); world-scope flat health | health.world.flat | modifier — world deposit |
| iWorldTradeRoutes | processProjectChange WORLD branch (CvTeam.cpp:4572-4574, every alive player); feeds getTradeRoutes (CvCity.cpp:15347) game+player+coastal+extra clamp | tradeRoutes.world.flat | modifier — world deposit |
| CommerceModifiers (named-array gold/research/culture/espionage) | processProjectChange empire branch (4559-4570); per-player getCommerceRateModifier feeds getTotalCommerceRateModifier (CvCity.cpp ~12008) -> getCommerceRateTimes100 | gold.empire.percent / research.empire.percent / culture.empire.percent / espionage.empire.percent (SPLIT per-identifier commerce families) | modifier — empire deposit (commerce SPLIT family, §70) |
| BonusProductionModifiers (keyed bonus->iVal) | getBonusProductionModifier; bonus presence shrinks build cost of THIS project via CvCity::getProductionModifier (build-faster-while-bonus-present) | buildRate.self.percent[].{value, enabled:{type:BONUS_X, scope:city, min:1}} | modifier — plots/units-target self build-rate, gated by bonus-present enabled atom (buildRate family) |
| iCost | intrinsic base hammer cost; the universal costs family (GameSpeed/Era costs.world.create.percent) MULTIPLIES this base read from the info | cost.create | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — base cost the costs modifier family scales |
| VictoryThresholds (keyed per-victory ints) | per-victory launch parameter read by victory resolution (CvGame::testVictory / CvTeam launch path), NOT an additive modifier family | victory.thresholds.{VICTORY_X: n} | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — structural victory section |
| VictoryMinThresholds (keyed per-victory ints) | per-victory minimum launch parameter read by victory resolution | victory.minThresholds.{VICTORY_X: n} | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — structural victory section |

| iSuccessRate | CvTeam::getLaunchSuccessRate; spaceship/launch success parameter | victory.successRate | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — structural victory section |
| EveryoneSpecialUnit | processProjectChange completion; one-shot makeSpecialUnitValid (every player may build the special unit) | grants.grantsSpecialUnit | grants — one-shot on completion |
| EveryoneSpecialBuilding | processProjectChange completion; one-shot makeSpecialBuildingValid (every player may build the special building) | grants.grantsSpecialBuilding | grants — one-shot on completion |
| TechPrereq | CvProjectInfo::getTechPrereq; build-availability tech gate (canCreate); store inverts onto the tech | DROP on project; store-inverted to tech.enables.projects on the prereq tech | enabler — enables (source-side, authored on the TECH not the project; derived edge) |
| PrereqProjects | CvProjectInfo::getPrereqProject; project->project build gate (e.g. SS_* parts need PROJECT_APOLLO_PROGRAM); store inverts | enables.projects (emitted on THIS project via store.enabled_by) | enabler — enables (source-side; this project's completion enables the gated projects) |
| VictoryPrereq | the victory this project's CREATION launches (not a build prereq); reverse 'launchedBy' is a cold-path derived edge | identity.launchesVictory | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iMaxGlobalInstances | CvProjectInfo::getMaxGlobalInstances; per-world build cap (canCreate count check) | identity.maxGlobalInstances | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| iMaxTeamInstances | CvProjectInfo::getMaxTeamInstances; per-team build cap (canCreate count check) | identity.maxTeamInstances | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| MapCategoryTypes | CvProjectInfo map-category eligibility list | identity.mapCategories | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| Categories | CvProjectInfo category tags | identity.categories | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| bSpaceship / bAllowsNukes / b* capability flags | CvProjectInfo capability getters (e.g. isSpaceship, isAllowsNukes); bool capability flags | identity.<camelCase flag> = true (b-stripped, first char lowercased) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| Description | display text (TXT_KEY) | description | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| Civilopedia | pedia text (TXT_KEY) | civilopedia | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| Button | CvProjectInfo art button path | ui.art.button (via ART_BLOCK) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| MovieDefineTag | CvProjectInfo movie define tag (completion movie) | ui.art.movie.defineTag (via ART_BLOCK) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| CreateSound | audio played when the project is COMPLETED (CvCity.cpp:16205) — named by what it is, not 'create' | sound.onCompletion (via ART_BLOCK) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| YieldModifiers | DEAD: CvProjectInfo has no YieldModifier member/getter/consumer; element never read | DROP (never authored) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — dead structure, dropped |
| bTechShareWithHalfCivs | DEAD: no consumer outside CvProjectInfo's own getter/checksum | DROP (never authored) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — dead structure, dropped |
| AnyonePrereqProject | CvProjectInfo::getAnyonePrereqProject; unused in current data (would need its own store edge if ever set) | DROP (never authored; would invert to an enables edge) | enabler — enables (derived edge; currently unused/dropped) |
| PrereqProjects/iNeeded (per-edge count) | per-prereq-edge needed-count (all 1 today); the set-based enables index does NOT carry count | NOT carried (set-based enables.projects has no count edge) | enabler — enables (count component not represented; see flags) |

### PromotionLine

| OLD XML | MEANS (legacy C++) | NEW JSON | cascade machine |
|---|---|---|---|
| Description / Help | getDescription() / getHelp() — display strings, not consumed by any gameplay getter | description / help | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) |
| OnGameOptions (child Type-strings) | getOnGameOption()/getNumOnGameOptionTypes() — load-stable game-option availability gate; engine removes the line's promotions when a listed GAMEOPTION is off (WorldBuilder toggle is the enable/disable mechanism, enabler.md §line206 LOAD-STABLE→PRUNE axis) | loadPrune.onGameOptions | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — loadPrune is the load-prune axis (data-model.md Auxiliary structural row), NOT the runtime enabler two-pass |
| NotOnGameOptions | (0 populated in data; getter pair would mirror getOnGameOption) — negative load-prune gate | loadPrune.notOnGameOptions | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — loadPrune axis; 0 populated so emits nothing in practice |
| bBuildUp | isBuildUp() — the live build-up-mechanic flag (28 lines); marks the line as a build-up chain | buildUp.active: true (object-module; presence+non-empty = active) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — buildUp is a dedicated OBJECT MODULE listed under data-model.md Auxiliary structural / bespoke; read by its own build-up system, does not cascade. Interim 'active' marker until the Promotion pass enriches it |
| UnitCombatPrereqTypes (child Type-strings) | getUnitCombatPrereqType()/getNumUnitCombatPrereqTypes() — read by CvPromotionInfo (cpp:2080) + CvUnit (17664/25585) to decide which units may take the line's promotions (unit-plane applicability gate) | identity.unitCombats | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — PARKED in identity, unit-plane enabling deferred/re-homed at the Promotion pass (#28); not store-inverted, kept faithfully here |
| NotOnDomainTypes (child Type-strings) | getNotOnDomainType()/getNumNotOnDomainTypes() — excluded-domain applicability gate, read by the same CvPromotionInfo/CvUnit consumers | identity.notOnDomains | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — PARKED, deferred to Promotion pass |
| NotOnUnitCombatTypes (child Type-strings) | getNotOnUnitCombatType()/getNumNotOnUnitCombatTypes() — excluded-unit-combat applicability gate, same consumers | identity.notOnUnitCombats | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — PARKED, deferred to Promotion pass |
| Button | getButton() — promotion-line button art FK (UI only) | ui.art.icon | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — UI art, not a cascade input |
| PrereqTech | getPrereqTech() — single prereq tech (1 line, TECH_PIERCING); single tech needs no AND-confirm | (store inversion) tech.enables.promotionLines — emitted on the TECH side, NOT on the PromotionLine JSON (PromotionLineInfo registered in store PREREQ_FIELDS) | enabler — enables (the inverted edge lives on Tech's enables; a lone tech → no requires per owner #4) |

| SHARED CAP_BOOL (imported from curate_promotion: bBlitz/bAmphib/bRiver/bEnemyRoute/bAlwaysHeal/bHillsDoubleMove/bImmuneToFirstStrikes/bDefensiveVictoryMove/bFreeDrop/bOffensiveVictoryMove/bOneUp/bPillage*/bCanMovePeaks/bCanLeadThroughPeaks/bZoneOfControl/bOnslaughtChange/bParalyze/bNoSelfHeal) — VERIFIED present in UnitCombat XML: bImmuneToFirstStrikes(UNITCOMBAT_STRIKE_TEAM), bFreeDrop(UNITCOMBAT_FELINE x2) | ⟨+verify⟩ | capabilities.{immuneToFirstStrikes, freeDrop, ...}: true | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — capabilities boolean group (grant=true); NOTE the slice docs UC-specific caps as 'modifier — capability boolean', which is inconsistent with how Promotion classes the IDENTICAL shared caps |
| SHARED CAP_PAIR (imported from curate_promotion: bStampedeChange/bRemoveStampede, bAttackOnlyCitiesAdd/Subtract, bIgnoreNoEntryLevelAdd/Subtract, bIgnoreZoneofControlAdd/Subtract, bFliesToMoveAdd/Subtract) — VERIFIED present in UnitCombat XML: bIgnoreZoneofControlAdd(UNITCOMBAT_EXILE x1), bFliesToMoveAdd(UNITCOMBAT_BALLOON x6) | ⟨+verify⟩ | capabilities.{ignoreZoneofControl, fliesToMove, ...}: true (Add->true / Subtract\|Remove->false) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — capabilities boolean grant/revoke pair group |
| SHARED CAP_COUNT (imported from curate_promotion: iNoDefensiveBonusChange/iGatherHerdChange/iAnimalIgnoresBordersChange/iExcileChange/iPassageChange/iNoNonOwnedCityEntryChange/iBarbCoExistChange/iBlendIntoCityChange/iUpgradeAnywhereChange/iHiddenNationalityChange/iAssassinChange/iStealthDefenseChange/iDefenseOnlyChange/iNoInvisibilityChange/iTriggerBeforeAttackChange) — VERIFIED present in UnitCombat XML: iGatherHerdChange, iAnimalIgnoresBordersChange, iExcileChange, iBarbCoExistChange, iStealthDefenseChange(x8), iDefenseOnlyChange, iNoInvisibilityChange | ⟨+verify⟩ | capabilities.{gatherHerd, animalIgnoresBorders, excile, barbCoExist, stealthDefense, defenseOnly, noInvisibility, ...}: true | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — capabilities boolean group (count truthy => grant). NB curator quirk: Python `if v:` makes NEGATIVE counts (e.g. -1, -2) ALSO emit true — iAnimalIgnoresBordersChange=-1, iDefenseOnlyChange=-1, iStealthDefenseChange=-1/-2 all emit capabilities.{name}:true |

| bValid | isValid (CvSpecialBuildingInfo) — m_bValid; when 0 the whole group is disabled (rare). | identity.valid: false (curate_special; only emitted when bValid is 0/false) | identity / auxiliary (non-cascade) — group-disable flag |

| TechPrereq | the legacy group tech-gate inherited by MEMBER buildings — CvPlayer::canConstruct (~6599) required the group's TechPrereq of any member that lacked its own (monasteries/temples/cathedrals/punk lines). Stored as m_iTechPrereq (read order in getDataMembers). | (SpecialBuilding side: DROPPED from the group JSON.) Routed two ways: (a) store enables index tech.enables.specialBuildings (store.py:171 ENABLE_FIELDS); (b) curator pushes it onto each member building's requires.build.all as a team-scope TECH confirm atom (curate_building.requires_building lines 748-754). | enabler — enables (source-side specialBuildings frontier) + the inherited member-building requires.build gate |
| TechPrereqAnyone | getTechPrereqAnyone (CvSpecialBuildingInfo) — m_iTechPrereqAnyone; the 'any team member has the tech' variant of the group gate. | (DROPPED from group JSON.) store enables index tech.enables.specialBuildings (store.py:172). UNUSED across all 36 groups -> the curator's member-gate inheritance skips it (curate_building note line 746). | enabler — enables (source-side specialBuildings frontier); latent/unused today |
| ObsoleteTech | getObsoleteTech (CvSpecialBuildingInfo) — m_iObsoleteTech; the tech that obsoletes the group, inherited by its member buildings (1 group today: SPECIALBUILDING_MONASTERY -> TECH_MODERN_PHYSICS). | (DROPPED from group JSON; NOT in store OBSOLETE_FIELDS.) store._inherit_group_obsoletes (store.py:329-345) expands it onto each member building -> tech.obsoletes.buildings keyed by the group's ObsoleteTech. | enabler — obsoletes (destructive edge; tech obsoletes the member buildings via group obsoletion inheritance) |
| (reverse-derived: any entity that references this SPECIALBUILDING as a source — e.g. via store enables family) | store.enabled_by(typ) — the forward reverse-index of what HAVING this group enables for other entities (enabler-spec §5/§6 enables family, CAN GET = enables - destructive over HAS). | enables: {bucket: [sorted types]} (curate_special out["enables"], only when non-empty) | enabler — enables (source-side, this group's contribution to the candidate frontier) |
| (reverse-derived: ObsoleteTech/obsoletion edges where this SPECIALBUILDING is the obsoleting source) | store.obsoletes_of(typ) — the forward reverse-index of what HAVING this group obsoletes (enabler-spec §6 destructive edge). | obsoletes: {bucket: [sorted types]} (curate_special out["obsoletes"], only when non-empty) | enabler — obsoletes (source-side destructive edge) |

## Needs owner ruling / ungrounded (flags)

- **Handicap:** iAIWarWearinessPercent — DUAL-CLASSIFIABLE: handicaps.md #423 table classes it MODIFIER* (game-scope AI-only % on war-weariness accumulation, an ongoing scalar), but the curator groups it under the diplomacy FAMILY alongside behavioural fields (declareWar/attitude/tech-trade). Its newJson (diplomacy.empire.warWeariness.ai.percent) and shape (% deposit) are unambiguous; only the machine-role label is judgement-dependent (economic-modifier vs diplo-behaviour grouping). Surfaced rather than silently picked — owner ruling whether the diplomacy family's AI-economy member is a true modifier deposit or non-cascade behaviour.
- **Handicap:** barbarians.* / combat.* / diplomacy.* family machine-role — these families carry game-rule / behavioural / combat-probability fields that handicaps.md #423 verdict explicitly rules OUT of the modifier cascade ('No — game-global rules / combat-rule probabilities / behavioural-diplo'). I labelled them 'identity / auxiliary (non-cascade)' on that grounding, but the curator emits them in family sections shaped exactly like modifier families (<family>.<scope>.<member>[.ai].<unit>) WITHOUT a `modifiers`/`identity` wrapper (flat-family structure). Whether these flat families are consumed by the cascade modifier machine or are config read by their legacy game-rule sites (CvGame/CvUnit/CvTeamAI) is an architectural call the handicap curator docstring defers to the FUTURE handicap-structure rework (out of #428 scope) — flagged so the machine column is not over-asserted as a cascade tenant.
- **Handicap:** PROPERTY_* family scope/unit — the exact scope (city/plot/unit) and unit (flat/percent/per/enabled) of each emitted PROPERTY_* family depend on the live PropertySource data per handicap record (resolved at curate time by engine.property_source_v3 from GameObjectType + PropertySourceType + the Active BoolExpr). I did not enumerate per-property-type rows because the concrete set/scopes are data-driven, not statically declared in the curator's FAMILIES table. The conversion rule is grounded; the per-instance leaves are not statically knowable from the curator alone. The curator docstring itself flags this as a deferred property-shape mismatch (#429 reconciliation).
- **Handicap:** perEra machine role — handicaps.md #423 verdict is 'No — meta-modifier-on-modifiers (era ramp)', i.e. it does NOT cleanly fit the modifier cascade (the cascade can't yet model a multiplier on a whole family × era). I labelled it 'modifier — empire deposit, ai audience' on the curator's family grouping, but it is the least-natural field, explicitly flagged for the future rework; whether it lands as a real modifier deposit or a special meta-channel is undecided.
- **Era:** bNoGoodies / bNoBarbUnits / bNoBarbCities — LIVE C++ world-RULE gates (goody placement CvMapGenerator.cpp:772, barb-unit spawn CvGame.cpp:7180, barb-city spawn CvGame.cpp:6941) but 0/absent in EVERY current era -> NOT emitted (zero-drop). They are NOT identity, NOT a modifier family, and have NO cascade machine home yet (the Vote pass did NOT resolve them; a world-state-gate concept needs separate planning). Ungroundable to a cascade machine -> no row. If an era ever sets one it surfaces in the curator leftover report (curate.curate else-branch lands a true b-flag in identity via de_i).
- **Era:** Type — the legacy <Type> identity key (ERA_*) is the record id, skipped by the curator (tag=='Type' continue) and re-emitted as out['type']; it is the entity key, not a migrated field/machine input.
- **Era:** maintenance.city.initial.flat (iInitialCityMaintenancePercent) — mapped as a city-scope modifier deposit per the curator FAMILIES table, but its legacy consumption is documented only as eraInitialPercent in the maintenance decomposition (legacy-value-calc-map §5/§12 notes the maintenance dump has NO decomposition emitted today); value is 0 in every era so it is zero-dropped. Mapping grounded in curator; live decomposition site not separately confirmed beyond the calc-map note.
- **Victory:** iTotalCultureRatio / getTotalCultureRatio() — DORMANT mechanic: a live engine getter exists but NO XML record sets <iTotalCultureRatio>, so the curator never emits it (no field reads the tag). Documented in the curator docstring as a faithful quirk; not a curated row because there is no source tag to map. Owner ruling needed only if a future record introduces it.
- **Victory:** VICTORY_SCIENTIFIC carries NO condition fields at all — its trigger is hardcoded / space-race-adjacent, lives outside VictoryInfo. Not a missing field; an entity-level note (curator docstring). No XML->JSON row to emit for it.
- **Vote:** iStateReligionVotePercent (threshold.stateReligionPercent) — getter getStateReligionVotePercent CONFIRMED in CvVoteInfo.h:24, but its consumption site was not visible in the grepped CvGame.cpp window; MEANS grounded on the curator docstring (per-player vote-weight bonus when state religion matches) + the confirmed getter, not on an observed call site. Not a cascade field either way; flagged for honest call-site verification, not as a mapping doubt.
- **Vote:** mode.cityVoting / mode.civVoting (bCityVoting/bCivVoting) — getters CONFIRMED in CvVoteInfo.h:32-33 and curator documents the consumer as CvPlayer::getVotes, but the exact getVotes consumption logic was not opened in this scoped pass; MEANS grounded on the curator docstring + confirmed getters. All-zero in current data (kept live, not dropped). Non-cascade regardless.
- **CultureLevel:** m_iLevel — RUNTIME-derived (no XML tag, computed active level); NOT a migrated field, intentionally not emitted by the curator. Listed for completeness, not a row.
- **CultureLevel:** SpeedThreshold col-2 getter name nuance: the engine getter is getSpeedThreshold(GameSpeedType) on CultureLevelInfo (called inside CvGame::getCultureThreshold, CvGame.cpp:9296). Grounded against engine source; the legacy-value-calc-map.md has no CultureLevel-specific getter entry (only a passing mention at line 185), so col-2 is grounded against the engine source + curator docstring rather than that doc.
- **CultureLevel:** defense.amount machine role: classified as a city-scope modifier deposit per the curator docstring + migration-renames (the one live additive modifier) and modifier.md city-scope vocabulary; the exact modifier.md family-registry line for `defense` was not re-read in this scoped pass — grounded via curator authority (owner ruling 2026-06-14 cited inline) not a direct modifier.md quote.
- **CultureLevel:** allowed.* machine role (tally vs enabler): migration-renames §165 states the canDoStuff ENABLER gate enforces the cap by READING THE TALLY (count atom). Row tags it `tally` as the count-atom source feeding that gate; if the owner prefers it tagged as the enabler-side `requires`-style cap consumer, that is a labeling ruling — flagged rather than guessed.
- **Hurry:** No fields ungroundable — all 3 migrated fields (iGoldPerProduction, iProductionPerPopulation, bAnger) are grounded against named CvCity getters/sites.
- **Hurry:** MACHINE-ROLE NOTE (not a fabrication, an owner-home call): all 3 Hurry fields are classified identity/auxiliary (non-cascade). The curator docstring (curate_hurry.py:5-12) explicitly rules they are NOT deposited modifiers and NOT enabler/tally atoms — they are the hurry's intrinsic mechanic parameters (the BASE rush rates the cascade BuildingInfo.hurryCostModifier acts on, plus the anger on/off flag). They drive real city/AI logic (CvCity canHurry gate, cost/yield calc, anger duration; CvPlayer pop-rush tracking; CvPlayerAI civic value) but none is a cascade-machine consumer ATOM. If the doc owner wants a 'config consumed-by-modifier' role distinct from plain identity, that is an owner-home decision.
- **Hurry:** STANDARD identity fields (Type, Description, Button->ui.art.icon) are present per curator but are standard de-Hungarianized identity, not migrated cascade fields — excluded from the table per the migrated-field scope; flagged here for completeness.
- **BonusClass:** TYPE/identity — the BonusClass record carries NO Description and (per curator docstring + CvBonusClassInfo.h) exactly ONE data field (m_iUniqueRange). The 'not placed on map' producer-class semantic (MANUFACTURED/CULTURE/GENMODS/WONDER) is HARDCODED in consumers (CvGameTextMgr filter) + XML comments, NOT a class field, so it is correctly NOT emitted — no row. The categorization itself is consumed BONUS-side via bonusClassType (already migrated on the Bonus entity), not on the class.
- **BonusClass:** machine-column — uniqueRange has NO consuming cascade machine. It is grounded as auxiliary/non-cascade (CvMapGenerator world-gen). Flagged for owner ruling only if the Rosetta doc requires every row to name one of the cascade machines; here the honest grounded answer is 'none — map-gen auxiliary'.
- **CivicOption (curate_civicoption.py — EntityConfig("CivicOptionInfo"), thin config over curate_common, empty boosts):** m_bPolicy / isPolicy() — DEAD C++ member: declared on CvCivicOptionInfo (Sources/Infos/CvCivicOptionInfo.h:36) but set by NO XML tag (no <bPolicy>/<Policy> in CIV4CivicOptionInfos.xml) and shadowed by the per-civic isPolicy. Absent from data -> no migrated row (nothing to drop), per curator docstring. Flagged for completeness, not a fabricated row.
- **CivicOption (curate_civicoption.py — EntityConfig("CivicOptionInfo"), thin config over curate_common, empty boosts):** m_pabTraitNoUpkeep / getTraitNoUpkeep — DEAD: the member exists but its getter is commented out in the header (CvCivicOptionInfo.h:25) and no <TraitNoUpkeep> tag appears in the XML. Not read, not migrated. Flagged for completeness.
- **CivicOption (curate_civicoption.py — EntityConfig("CivicOptionInfo"), thin config over curate_common, empty boosts):** COVERAGE — CivicOption migrates EXACTLY two fields (Type, Description); the XML authors no others (verified across all 15 records in CIV4CivicOptionInfos.xml, all of form <Type>+<Description>) and the JSON output carries only {type, description} (verified civicoption_government.json). No modifier/enabler/tally/grants field exists on this entity, so no rows are owed in those machines.
- **Civilization:** Type (XML <Type>) — the record key/identifier, emitted by the curator as the top-level `type` field and as the per-civ filename; it is the entity identity primitive, not a migrated value-bearing field, so it is intentionally not given a 4-column data row (it is in the curator's KNOWN set as the row key, not a mapped field).
- **Civilization:** iSpawnRateModifier / iSpawnRateNPCPeaceModifier machine-role nuance — modeled in the curator under `spawnRate.empire.*.percent` and called 'the ONE cascade modifier' in the curator docstring/rename-registry, BUT legacy-value-calc-map §10.4 explicitly classes spawnRate as a STOCHASTIC per-plot RNG event 'NOT a per-turn value-channel' (closer to a grants.repeatable chance). Row col4 reflects the curator's `modifier — empire deposit` framing; owner ruling may prefer it routed out of the modifier channel into a probability/spawn sub-system. Surfaced rather than silently picking one.
- **Tech:** grants.firstFreeUnit / grants.firstFreeProphet / grants.freeTechs — GROUNDED as grants (curate_tech.py GRANTS dict + getFirstFreeUnit/getFirstFreeProphet/getFirstFreeTechs getters), but migration-renames §Tech explicitly FLAGS these as differing from enabler-spec §6 plain grants.{units,techs} lists: they carry first-TO-RESEARCH race semantics pending the §10-banked firstToResearch predicate. Owner call whether to restructure later — not a fabrication, an open structural question.
- **Tech:** identity.disable (bDisable) — GROUNDED (isDisable getter, only TECH_DUMMY), but migration-renames flags it as a LIVE unconditional research kill that is arguably loadPrune-like yet doesn't fit loadPrune's {onGameOptions} shape; kept in identity pending an OWNER HOME DECISION. Its true cascade machine (enabler disable vs loadPrune vs identity) is unresolved.
- **Tech:** RouteInfo.TechMovementChanges → movement.team.routes — GROUNDED (getTechMovementChange getter + TECH_BOOSTS row), but the curator's own comment + migration-renames FLAG it for follow-up: it belongs in the moveCost family ON THE ROUTE (movement subsystem §6.6), currently still inverted onto the tech (harmless, unconsumed). Its final machine/home is deferred.
- **Tech:** Trade-enabler bool channels (bTechTrading … bDCMAirBombTech2, TerrainTrades, CommerceFlexible) — col 2 getters GROUNDED in CvTechInfo.h, and they route through apply_channel's kind:enabler path into top-level capability families. They are TEAM/empire-scope CAPABILITY FLAGS (boolean grants of an ability), NOT numeric modifier deposits; I classified them as 'modifier — team/empire deposit (enabler capability flag)' because the curator emits them as flat family sections, but whether a boolean capability is canonically a 'modifier' or its own 'capability/grant' atom in the cascade vocab is not nailed in the scoped docs (modifier.md/enabler.md not read in full for this boolean-capability case). Owner/spec ruling may reclassify.
- **Tech:** BuildingInfo.TechSpecialistChanges (2D-keyed building×specialist) → freeSpecialists.city.buildings.{BUILDING}.specialists.{SPECIALIST}.flat — documented in migration-renames §Tech as a downward deposit onto the tech via the optional 8th subTargetType TECH_BOOSTS field, BUT the live curate_tech.py TECH_BOOSTS table does NOT contain this row (only the RouteInfo TechMovementChanges row remains; the docstring says the keyed+tech-enabled combo is FLAGGED as a COND_KEYED follow-up belonging on the building). The land-atom path is currently never exercised by live data; correctness of the land branch is unverified against a real land building.

- **SpecialBuilding:** TechPrereqAnyone — grounded as routed to store tech.enables.specialBuildings (store.py:172) but the curator (curate_building line 746) documents it as UNUSED across all 36 groups; so its enabler contribution is latent/empty today, not a live edge. - **SpecialBuilding:** enables/obsoletes (curate_special out keys) — these are reverse-DERIVED forward indices (store.enabled_by/obsoletes_of), NOT a 1:1 XML tag on the SpecialBuilding record; their CONTENT comes from other entities referencing this group. Listed as rows because curate_special emits them, but their oldXml source is the referrer side, not a SpecialBuilding tag — flagged so the owner confirms how to render a derived-index column in the Rosetta table.
- **SpecialBuilding:** ObsoleteTech routing — confirmed it is deliberately NOT in store OBSOLETE_FIELDS (store.py:187-194 lists BuildingInfo/UnitInfo/etc. but NOT SpecialBuildingInfo); instead handled by the bespoke _inherit_group_obsoletes pushing onto member buildings. Flagged because this is an asymmetric/special path vs every other entity's ObsoleteTech, easy to mis-map as a normal tech.obsoletes.specialBuildings edge (which does NOT exist).
- **Unit:** bMilitaryProduction / bMilitarySupport / bMilitaryTrade / bMilitaryHappiness — the curator maps these as plain capability BOOLEANS (capabilities.military*), but the legacy engine reads bMilitaryProduction/bMilitaryHappiness as inputs to CITY-LEVEL accumulators (military-happiness byOccupant, military production gating). modifier.md §5 names byOccupant as the host-scope cross-edge for military-happiness, so whether these stay boolean capabilities or become byOccupant/host-scope deposits is an owner ruling — the curator currently treats them as identity capabilities only.
- **Unit:** iInstanceCostModifier scope — curator emits per:{type:SELF, scope:empire} INSIDE costs.empire.perInstance, but legacy getInstanceCostModifier ramps on the count of existing instances; the migration-renames row only shows per:{type:SELF} without restating the scope. Confirmed empire from the _atom('SELF','empire') call, but the count-domain (player vs team vs world lifetime) for the cost ramp is not separately documented — owner-confirm the SELF count scope matches legacy.
- **Unit:** UnitMeshGroups / FreePromotions in HANDLED-extra set — UnitMeshGroups is swept into HANDLED only for coverage (art/graphics, no JSON emission documented); FreePromotions appears BOTH in GRANT_LIST (grants.promotions) and the HANDLED-extra set — the extra-set listing is a coverage dup, the real mapping is grants.promotions (mapped above).
- **Unit:** DefendAgainstUnit double-listed — appears in both ID_LIST (-> identity.defendAgainstUnit) and PASS2_TAGS; mapped here to identity per ID_LIST, but its PASS2 listing implies a possible future combat-resolution role. Flagged as ambiguous until the #430 combat pass.
- **Unit:** base/deposit boundary (iCombat/iMoves/iWorkRate/iAirCombat/iCargo/iCombatLimit) — the curator docstring itself flags this split as 'a judgement, flagged for owner inspection'. Mapped to identity.base per current curator, but owner may reclassify some as modifier deposits.
- **SpecialUnit (curate_unit.py :: curate_special_unit; SpecialUnitInfo, 7 records, written to Assets/Data/specialunits/):** iCombatPercent (oldXml; present on SPECIALUNIT_CAPTIVE only, 1 record) -- HAS a real engine deposit: getCombatPercent() -> CvUnit::processLoadedSpecialUnit (CvUnit.cpp:28856) calls changeExtraCombatPercent on the LOADED unit, i.e. a strength modifier deposited onto the carried unit; and Tools/Migration/mapping/SpecialUnitInfo.json maps it {scope:city, channel:combat, kind:percent}. BUT the AUTHORITATIVE curate_special_unit() does NOT emit it (it only handles bValid/bCityLoad/bSMLoadSame/Description). The curator docstring (line 23-24) explicitly says SpecialUnit should include 'any combat deposits onto the loaded unit' -- so this is a real un-migrated gap, not a fabricated row. Would-be target: a strength family deposit (modifier.md strength family) onto the loaded unit. NEEDS OWNER/CURATOR ruling: emit it, or confirm intentionally deferred.
- **SpecialUnit (curate_unit.py :: curate_special_unit; SpecialUnitInfo, 7 records, written to Assets/Data/specialunits/):** iWithdrawalChange (oldXml; present on SPECIALUNIT_CAPTIVE only, 1 record) -- same situation: getWithdrawalChange() -> processLoadedSpecialUnit (CvUnit.cpp:28857) calls changeExtraWithdrawal on the LOADED unit (a withdrawal-family modifier on the carried unit); mapping/SpecialUnitInfo.json maps it {scope:city, channel:withdrawal, kind:flat}. NOT emitted by curate_special_unit(). Would-be target: a withdrawal family deposit (modifier.md withdrawal family) onto the loaded unit. NEEDS OWNER/CURATOR ruling: emit it, or confirm intentionally deferred.
